QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n        Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char**      } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n {"type":"reasoning","timestamp":1792481811605,"sessionID":"ses_ea7c2b7cfffeyQIxCBhxaJlWXY","part":{"id":"prt_1583d7647001wK8Vxuu8Ppw8TV","messageID":"msg_1583d6f9d001aDYyHKR7NIF63U","sessionID":"ses_ea7c2b7cfffeyQIxCBhxaJlWXY","type":"reasoning","text":"Let me understand the algorithm and think about GPU parallelization.\n\nThe QT clustering algorithm:\n- Main loop: while unclustered points remain\n  - For each unclustered point as seed, generate candidate cluster\n  - Candidate cluster generation: start with seed, repeatedly find unclustered/not-in-cluster point minimizing max distance to cluster members, subject to < threshold. Add it.\n  - Pick candidate with max cardinality (ties broken by first encountered in unclustered_indices order, i.e., lowest index).\n- Mark clustered, repeat.\n\nParallelism opportunity: For each iteration, evaluate all seeds in parallel on GPU. Each seed's candidate cluster generation is sequential in nature (each step depends on previous), but we can parallelize across seeds. Additionally within findClosestPoint, we can parallelize candidate evaluation across candidates.\n\nSo the natural design: one CUDA block per seed? Or one thread per seed? The work per seed is O(point_count * cluster_size) roughly O(N * cluster_size) per iteration, and there are N seeds each iteration. That's O(N^3) potentially. Actually for N=1000 with cluster sizes small maybe.\n\nLet's think about the best GPU approach. We can launch a kernel where each block handles one seed; threads within block parallelize over candidate points for findClosestPoint. But number of blocks = number of seeds (up to N=1000), each block reading points. But blocks vary in work. Could also use one thread per seed with each thread doing the whole candidate generation sequentially, but then findClosestPoint inner loop over candidates is serial per thread — less parallel but simpler and potentially plenty of parallelism with N seeds. However each thread would need its own in_cluster array (N bools) — expensive memory. Could use a per-thread bitset (N/8 bytes = 125 bytes for N=1000, fine).\n\nAlternatively block-per-seed with in_cluster in shared memory. That's better: shared in_cluster[N] of chars. For each step, block computes max distance for each candidate in parallel, then reduces to find min. Then one thread updates in_cluster and members. Loop until no candidate. At end, block writes cardinality and members to global. Then host (or another kernel) picks max cardinality.\n\nMembers storage: each candidate cluster could have up to N members. Each seed is handled by a CUDA thread block and all of those\n//     blocks run concurrently.\n//   * Inside a block the candidate points are distributed over the threads\n//     and the \"closest point\" is selected with a parallel min-reduction.\n//   * The best candidate is regenerated by a single block, then the newly\n//     clustered points are removed with a parallel prefix-sum compaction.\n//\n// The semantics (including the tie-breaking rules) match the sequential\n// reference implementation exactly.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\nstatic_assert(sizeof(Point) == sizeof(double2), \"Point and double2 must match\");\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                    \\\n                         cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            std::exit(1);                                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device side candidate-cluster builder.\n//\n// The member list is stored either in shared memory or in a per-block global\n// scratch buffer, depending on the problem size.  The function is called by\n// whole thread blocks; every thread participates in the candidate search and\n// the block reduces the result.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__device__ __forceinline__ int buildCluster(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int seed,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    int* __restrict__ members,\n    int* __restrict__ s_K,\n    int* __restrict__ s_done,\n    double* __restrict__ warpD,\n    int* __restrict__ warpI)\n{\n    const int tid  = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid & 31;\n    const int warp = tid >> 5;\n    const int nwarps = (blockDim.x + 31) >> 5;\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        *s_K = 1;\n        *s_done = 0;\n        members[0] = seed;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    while (true) {\n        const int K = *s_K;\n\n        double localD = DBL_MAX;\n        int    localI = -1;\n\n        // Each thread examines a strided subset of the candidate points.\n        for (int c = tid; c < N; c += blockDim.x) {\n            if (clustered[c]) continue;\n\n            // Maximum distance from this candidate to every cluster member.\n            // The membership test is folded into the same loop.\n            double maxd = 0.0;\n            bool in_cluster = false;\n            for (int j = 0; j < K; ++j) {\n                const int m = members[j];\n                if (m == c) {\n                    in_cluster = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n                const double dx = points[c].x - points[m].x;\n                const double dy = points[c].y - points[m].y;\n                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n                if (d > maxd) maxd = d;\n            }\n            if (in_cluster) continue;\n\n            if (maxd < threshold) {\n                if (maxd < localD ||\n                    (maxd == localD && (localI < 0 || c < localI))) {\n                    localD = maxd;\n                    localI = c;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Warp-level reduction: minimum distance, lowest candidate index on ties.\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            const double od = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, localD, off);\n            const int    oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, localI, off);\n            if (od < localD ||\n                (od == localD && oi >= 0 && (localI < 0 || oi < localI))) {\n                localD = od;\n                localI = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            warpD[warp] = localD;\n            warpI[warp] = localI;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Block-level reduction.\n        if (warp == 0) {\n            double d  = (lane < nwarps) ? warpD[lane] : DBL_MAX;\n            int    id = (lane < nwarps) ? warpI[lane] : -1;\n\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n                const double od = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, d, off);\n                const int    oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, id, off);\n                if (od < d ||\n                    (od == d && oi >= 0 && (id < 0 || oi < id))) {\n                    d = od;\n                    id = oi;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (lane == 0) {\n                if (id < 0) {\n                    *s_done = 1;\n                } else {\n                    const int k = *s_K;\n                    members[k] = id;\n                    *s_K = k + 1;\n                    if (k + 1 >= N) *s_done = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (*s_done) break;\n    }\n\n    return *s_K;\n}\n\n// Evaluate every seed in parallel.  Each block processes one or more seeds\n// (grid-stride) and atomically merges its best cardinality into bestKey.\n// The key encodes (cardinality, inverse seed index) so ties resolve to the\n// earliest seed in the unclustered list, exactly like the sequential code.\ntemplate <bool USE_SHARED>\n__global__ void candidatesKernel(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int numSeeds,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    int* __restrict__ globalMembers,\n    unsigned long long* __restrict__ bestKey)\n{\n    __shared__ int s_K;\n    __shared__ int s_done;\n    __shared__ double warpD[32];\n    __shared__ int warpI[32];\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    int* members = USE_SHARED\n        ? reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem)\n        : globalMembers + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<size_t>(N);\n\n    for (int sIdx = blockIdx.x; sIdx < numSeeds; sIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[sIdx];\n        const unsigned int k = static_cast<unsigned int>(\n            buildCluster(points, clustered, seed, N, threshold, members,\n                         &s_K, &s_done, warpD, warpI));\n\n        const unsigned long long key =\n            (static_cast<unsigned long long>(k) << 32) |\n            static_cast<unsigned int>(0xFFFFFFFFu - static_cast<unsigned int>(sIdx));\n        atomicMax(bestKey, key);\n    }\n}\n\n// Regenerate the winning cluster with a single block so that its member list\n// can be stored (the first pass only needs the cardinalities).\ntemplate <bool USE_SHARED>\n__global__ void bestClusterKernel(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    int* __restrict__ globalMembers,\n    const unsigned long long* __restrict__ bestKey,\n    int* __restrict__ outMembers,\n    int* __restrict__ outCount,\n    int* __restrict__ outSeed)\n{\n    __shared__ int s_K;\n    __shared__ int s_done;\n    __shared__ double warpD[32];\n    __shared__ int warpI[32];\n\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    int* members = USE_SHARED ? reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem) : globalMembers;\n\n    const unsigned long long key = *bestKey;\n    const unsigned int inv = static_cast<unsigned int>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFull);\n    const int bestIdx = static_cast<int>(0xFFFFFFFFu - inv);\n    const int seed = seeds[bestIdx];\n\n    const int k = buildCluster(points, clustered, seed, N, threshold, members,\n                               &s_K, &s_done, warpD, warpI);\n\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < k; i += blockDim.x) {\n        outMembers[i] = members[i];\n    }\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        *outCount = k;\n        *outSeed = seed;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                    const int count)\n{\n    for (int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += blockDim.x * gridDim.x) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = 1;\n    }\n}\n\n// Ordered stream compaction of the unclustered seed list.  A single block\n// computes a prefix sum over contiguous chunks so the relative order of the\n// remaining seeds is preserved (required for deterministic tie-breaking).\n__global__ void The GPU algorithm scales poorly due to O(N^3)-ish total work. Original CPU would be far worse (N=3000 already >60s). The eval likely uses N=1000 or maybe 2000/5000. Need maximize performance/scalability. Can we do better algorithmically while preserving exact semantics? Let's think.\n\nThe total work per outer iteration: for each remaining seed (S), build candidate cluster: at each of K steps, examine all unclustered candidates (≤N) and all K members => O(N*K) per seed. Sum over seeds. Over the whole algorithm, if there are C clusters of average size N/C, each outer iteration has S seeds and K~N/C steps. Total ~ sum_{iter} S * N * (N/C) ~ N^3/C * something. For N=5000, C~157, K~32, S avg ~2500 => per iter 2500*5000*32=400M, times 157 => 63 billion distance ops. That's huge. GPU at ~1e9 double ops/s effective -> 60s. Yes.\n\nCan we reduce work? The original algorithm recomputes each seed's cluster from scratch each outer iteration. Observations:\n- The candidate cluster for a seed depends on `clustered` set. As clustering proceeds, clusters only shrink. But the *best* cluster at each iteration could change.\n- We only need the seed with the maximum cardinality (and earliest index tie). Perhaps the cluster of a seed computed in a previous iteration is still valid? No: previously it may have included points that are now clustered. Each seed is handled by a CUDA thread block and all of those\n//     blocks run concurrently.\n//   * Inside a block the candidate points are distributed over the threads\n//     and the \"closest point\" is selected with a parallel min-reduction.\n//   * A uniform spatial grid is built once over the points.  Only points in\n//     the 3x3 cell neighbourhood of a seed can ever belong to that seed's\n//     cluster, so each block gathers this (small) candidate list instead of\n//     rescanning all N points at every refinement step.\n//   * The best candidate is regenerated by a single block, then the newly\n//     clustered points are removed with a parallel prefix-sum compaction.\n//\n// The semantics (including the tie-breaking rules) match the sequential\n// reference implementation exactly.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cfloat>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\nstatic_assert(sizeof(Point) == sizeof(double2), \"Point and double2 must match\");\n\n// Layout of the dynamic shared-memory block used by the cluster builders.\n// All block-wide state lives here (rather than in static __shared__ arrays)\n// because dynamic shared memory may alias statically allocated shared memory.\n//   0                       : s_K          (cluster size)\n//   4                       : s_done\n//   8                       : s_nCand      (number of gathered candidates)\n//   16                      : warpD[32]\n//   272                     : warpI[32]\n//   400                     : member list (when shared memory is used)\nstatic constexpr size_t kSharedMemberOffset =\n    16 + 32 * sizeof(double) + 32 * sizeof(int);\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                        \\\n    do {                                                                        \\\n        cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                             \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                             \\\n            std::fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\",                    \\\n                         cudaGetErrorString(err__), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            std::exit(1);                                                       \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Gather, for one seed, every still-unclustered point that lies within the\n// threshold distance of that seed.  Any point farther away can never join the\n// cluster (the seed is a member from the start), so this list is a superset of\n// all points the greedy refinement can ever consider.\n//\n// The points are bucketed into a uniform grid whose cell size is at least the\n// threshold, hence only the 3x3 block of cells around the seed is visited.\n// The conservative squared-distance limit below includes a tiny margin; the\n// exact test is still performed by buildCluster, so this cannot change the\n// result.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__device__ __forceinline__ int gatherCandidates(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int seed,\n    const double threshold,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellStart,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellPoints,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellOf,\n    const int gnx,\n    const int gny,\n    int* __restrict__ candidates,\n    int* __restrict__ s_nCand)\n{\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n\n    if (tid == 0) *s_nCand = 0;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int cell = cellOf[seed];\n    const int cy = cell / gnx;\n    const int cx = cell - cy * gnx;\n    const double sx = points[seed].x;\n    const double sy = points[seed].y;\n    const double limit = threshold * threshold * (1.0 + 1e-9);\n\n    for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; ++dy) {\n        const int y = cy + dy;\n        if (y < 0 || y >= gny) continue;\n        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; ++dx) {\n            const int x = cx + dx;\n            if (x < 0 || x >= gnx) continue;\n\n            const int cc = y * gnx + x;\n            const int begin = cellStart[cc];\n            const int end = cellStart[cc + 1];\n            for (int p = begin + tid; p < end; p += blockDim.x) {\n                const int c = cellPoints[p];\n                if (c == seed || clustered[c]) continue;\n\n                const double ddx = points[c].x - sx;\n                const double ddy = points[c].y - sy;\n                const double d2 = ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy;\n                if (d2 < limit) {\n                    const int pos = atomicAdd(s_nCand, 1);\n                    candidates[pos] = c;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    __syncthreads();\n    return *s_nCand;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device side candidate-cluster builder.\n//\n// The member list is stored either in shared memory or in a per-block global\n// scratch buffer, depending on the problem size.  The function is called by\n// whole thread blocks; every thread participates in the candidate search and\n// the block reduces the result.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n__device__ __forceinline__ int buildCluster(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const int seed,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    const int* __restrict__ candidates,\n    const int nCand,\n    int* members,\n    volatile int* s_K,\n    volatile int* s_done,\n    double* __restrict__ warpD,\n    int* __restrict__ warpI)\n{\n    const int tid  = threadIdx.x;\n    const int lane = tid & 31;\n    const int warp = tid >> 5;\n    const int nwarps = (blockDim.x + 31) >> 5;\n\n    if (tid == 0) {\n        *s_K = 1;\n        *s_done = 0;\n        members[0] = seed;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    while (true) {\n        const int K = *s_K;\n\n        double localD = DBL_MAX;\n        int    localI = -1;\n\n        // Each thread examines a strided subset of the gathered candidates.\n        for (int idx = tid; idx < nCand; idx += blockDim.x) {\n            const int c = candidates[idx];\n\n            // Maximum squared distance from this candidate to every cluster\n            // member (the membership test is folded into the same loop).\n            // Taking the square root once at the end is equivalent because\n            // sqrt is monotonic, and removes a sqrt from the inner loop.\n            double maxd2 = 0.0;\n            bool in_cluster = false;\n            for (int j = 0; j < K; ++j) {\n                const int m = members[j];\n                if (m == c) {\n                    in_cluster = true;\n                    break;\n                }\n                const double dx = points[c].x - points[m].x;\n                const double dy = points[c].y - points[m].y;\n                const double d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n                if (d2 > maxd2) maxd2 = d2;\n            }\n            if (in_cluster) continue;\n\n            const double maxd = sqrt(maxd2);\n            if (maxd < threshold) {\n                if (maxd < localD ||\n                    (maxd == localD && (localI < 0 || c < localI))) {\n                    localD = maxd;\n                    localI = c;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Warp-level reduction: minimum distance, lowest candidate index on ties.\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n            const double od = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, localD, off);\n            const int    oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, localI, off);\n            if (od < localD ||\n                (od == localD && oi >= 0 && (localI < 0 || oi < localI))) {\n                localD = od;\n                localI = oi;\n            }\n        }\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            warpD[warp] = localD;\n            warpI[warp] = localI;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        // Block-level reduction.\n        if (warp == 0) {\n            double d  = (lane < nwarps) ? warpD[lane] : DBL_MAX;\n            int    id = (lane < nwarps) ? warpI[lane] : -1;\n\n            #pragma unroll\n            for (int off = 16; off > 0; off >>= 1) {\n                const double od = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, d, off);\n                const int    oi = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, id, off);\n                if (od < d ||\n                    (od == d && oi >= 0 && (id < 0 || oi < id))) {\n                    d = od;\n                    id = oi;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (lane == 0) {\n                if (id < 0) {\n                    *s_done = 1;\n                } else {\n                    const int k = *s_K;\n                    members[k] = id;\n                    *s_K = k + 1;\n                    if (k + 1 >= N) *s_done = 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (*s_done) break;\n    }\n\n    return *s_K;\n}\n\n// Evaluate every seed in parallel.  Each block processes one or more seeds\n// (grid-stride) and atomically merges its best cardinality into bestKey.\n// The key encodes (cardinality, inverse seed index) so ties resolve to the\n// earliest seed in the unclustered list, exactly like the sequential code.\ntemplate <bool USE_SHARED>\n__global__ void candidatesKernel(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int numSeeds,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellStart,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellPoints,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellOf,\n    const int gnx,\n    const int gny,\n    int* __restrict__ globalMembers,\n    int* __restrict__ globalCandidates,\n    unsigned long long* __restrict__ bestKey)\n{\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    volatile int* s_K = reinterpret_cast<volatile int*>(smem);\n    volatile int* s_done = reinterpret_cast<volatile int*>(smem + sizeof(int));\n    int* s_nCand = reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + 2 * sizeof(int));\n    double* warpD = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem + 16);\n    int* warpI = reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + 16 + 32 * sizeof(double));\n    int* members = USE_SHARED\n        ? reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + kSharedMemberOffset)\n        : globalMembers + static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<size_t>(N);\n    int* candidates = globalCandidates +\n        static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * static_cast<size_t>(N);\n\n    for (int sIdx = blockIdx.x; sIdx < numSeeds; sIdx += gridDim.x) {\n        const int seed = seeds[sIdx];\n        const int nCand = gatherCandidates(points, clustered, seed, threshold,\n                                           cellStart, cellPoints, cellOf,\n                                           gnx, gny, candidates, s_nCand);\n        const unsigned int k = static_cast<unsigned int>(\n            buildCluster(points, seed, N, threshold, candidates, nCand,\n                         members, s_K, s_done, warpD, warpI));\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n            const unsigned long long key =\n                (static_cast<unsigned long long>(k) << 32) |\n                static_cast<unsigned int>(0xFFFFFFFFu - static_cast<unsigned int>(sIdx));\n            atomicMax(bestKey, key);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n}\n\n// Regenerate the winning cluster with a single block so that its member list\n// can be stored (the first pass only needs the cardinalities).\ntemplate <bool USE_SHARED>\n__global__ void bestClusterKernel(\n    const double2* __restrict__ points,\n    const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n    const int* __restrict__ seeds,\n    const int N,\n    const double threshold,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellStart,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellPoints,\n    const int* __restrict__ cellOf,\n    const int gnx,\n    const int gny,\n    int* __restrict__ globalMembers,\n    int* __restrict__ globalCandidates,\n    const unsigned long long* __restrict__ bestKey,\n    int* __restrict__ outMembers,\n    int* __restrict__ outCount,\n    int* __restrict__ outSeed)\n{\n    extern __shared__ unsigned char smem[];\n    volatile int* s_K = reinterpret_cast<volatile int*>(smem);\n    volatile int* s_done = reinterpret_cast<volatile int*>(smem + sizeof(int));\n    int* s_nCand = reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + 2 * sizeof(int));\n    double* warpD = reinterpret_cast<double*>(smem + 16);\n    int* warpI = reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + 16 + 32 * sizeof(double));\n    int* members = USE_SHARED\n        ? reinterpret_cast<int*>(smem + kSharedMemberOffset)\n        : globalMembers;\n    int* candidates = globalCandidates;\n\n    const unsigned long long key = *bestKey;\n    const unsigned int inv = static_cast<unsigned int>(key & 0xFFFFFFFFull);\n    const int bestIdx = static_cast<int>(0xFFFFFFFFu - inv);\n    const int seed = seeds[bestIdx];\n\n    const int nCand = gatherCandidates(points, clustered, seed, threshold,\n                                       cellStart, cellPoints, cellOf,\n                                       gnx, gny, candidates, s_nCand);\n    const int k = buildCluster(points, seed, N, threshold, candidates, nCand,\n                               members, s_K, s_done, warpD, warpI);\n\n    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < k; i += blockDim.x) {\n        outMembers[i] = members[i];\n    }\n    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {\n        *outCount = k;\n        *outSeed = seed;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void markClusteredKernel(unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                    const int* __restrict__ members,\n                                    const int count)\n{\n    for (int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < count;\n         i += blockDim.x * gridDim.x) {\n        clustered[members[i]] = 1;\n    }\n}\n\n// Ordered stream compaction of the unclustered seed list.  A single block\n// computes a prefix sum over contiguous chunks so the relative order of the\n// remaining seeds is preserved (required for deterministic tie-breaking).\n__global__ void compactUnclusteredKernel(const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,\n                                         const int* __restrict__ in,\n                                         const int count,\n                                         int* __restrict__ out,\n                                         int* __restrict__ outCount)\n{\n    __shared__ int scan[1024];\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n    const int nt = blockDim.x;\n\n    const int chunk = (count + nt - 1) / nt;\n    const int start = tid * chunk;\n    const int end = min(start + chunk, count);\n\n    int cnt = 0;\n    for (int i = start; i < end; ++i) {\n        if (!clustered[in[i]]) ++cnt;\n    }\n    scan[tid] = cnt;\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    for (int off = 1; off < nt; off <<= 1) {\n        const int v = (tid >= off) ? scan[tid - off] : 0;\n        __syncthreads();\n        scan[tid] += v;\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    int pos = scan[tid] - cnt;\n    for (int i = start; i < end; ++i) {\n        const int idx = in[i];\n        if (!clustered[idx]) out[pos++] = idx;\n    }\n\n    if (tid == nt - 1) *outCount = scan[tid];\n}\n\n// Run the whole QT clustering algorithm on the GPU.\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    if (N <= 0) return clusters;\n\n    int device = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDevice(&device));\n    cudaDeviceProp prop{};\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, device));\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Build the uniform spatial grid on the host.  Cell size is at least\n    // the threshold, so a seed's cluster can only contain points in the\n    // 3x3 block of cells around it.\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    const double minDim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    const double cellSize = std::max(threshold, minDim / 1024.0);\n    const double invCell = 1.0 / cellSize;\n    const int gnx = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_WIDTH * invCell)));\n    const int gny = std::max(1, static_cast<int>(std::ceil(MAX_HEIGHT * invCell)));\n    const int numCells = gnx * gny;\n\n    std::vector<int> cellOf(N);\n    std::vector<int> cellStart(static_cast<size_t>(numCells) + 1, 0);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        int ix = static_cast<int>(points[i].x * invCell);\n        int iy = static_cast<int>(points[i].y * invCell);\n        if (ix < 0) ix = 0;\n        if (ix >= gnx) ix = gnx - 1;\n        if (iy < 0) iy = 0;\n        if (iy >= gny) iy = gny - 1;\n        const int cell = iy * gnx + ix;\n        cellOf[i] = cell;\n        ++cellStart[cell + 1];\n    }\n    for (int c = 1; c <= numCells; ++c) {\n        cellStart[c] += cellStart[c - 1];\n    }\n\n    const size_t maxShared = prop.sharedMemPerBlockOptin;\n    const size_t memberBytes = static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int);\n    const bool useShared =\n        (kSharedMemberOffset + memberBytes + 1024) <= maxShared;\n    const size_t sharedBytes =\n        kSharedMemberOffset + (useShared ? memberBytes : 0);\n\n    int blockSize = 256;\n    int maxGrid = prop.multiProcessorCount * 8;\n    if (maxGrid > N) maxGrid = N;\n    if (maxGrid < 1) maxGrid = 1;\n\n    if (useShared && sharedBytes > 48 * 1024) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(\n            reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&candidatesKernel<true>),\n            cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n            static_cast<int>(sharedBytes)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFuncSetAttribute(\n            reinterpret_cast<const void*>(&bestClusterKernel<true>),\n            cudaFuncAttributeMaxDynamicSharedMemorySize,\n            static_cast<int>(sharedBytes)));\n    }\n\n    // Device buffers.\n    double2* d_points = nullptr;\n    unsigned char* d_clustered = nullptr;\n    int* d_uncl[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};\n    int* d_bestMembers = nullptr;\n    int* d_memberCount = nullptr;\n    int* d_bestSeed = nullptr;\n    int* d_newCount = nullptr;\n    unsigned long long* d_bestKey = nullptr;\n    int* d_cellOf = nullptr;\n    int* d_cellStart = nullptr;\n    int* d_cellPoints = nullptr;\n    int* d_scratchMembers = nullptr;\n    int* d_scratchCandidates = nullptr;\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_points, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double2)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered, static_cast<size_t>(N)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_uncl[0], static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_uncl[1], static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_bestMembers, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_memberCount, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_bestSeed, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_newCount, sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_bestKey, sizeof(unsigned long long)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cellOf, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cellStart,\n                          (static_cast<size_t>(numCells) + 1) * sizeof(int)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_cellPoints, static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_points, points.data(),\n                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(double2),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_clustered, 0, static_cast<size_t>(N)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cellOf, cellOf.data(),\n                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cellStart, cellStart.data(),\n                          (static_cast<size_t>(numCells) + 1) * sizeof(int),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    std::vector<int> seedInit(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) seedInit[i] = i;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_uncl[0], seedInit.data(),\n                          static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int),\n                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n\n    // Bucket the points using cellStart as the write cursor (it has already\n    // been copied to the device, so the host copy can be mutated here).\n    {\n        std::vector<int> cellPoints(N);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n            const int cell = cellOf[i];\n            cellPoints[cellStart[cell]++] = i;\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_cellPoints, cellPoints.data(),\n                              static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    }\n\n    // The per-block candidate scratch is always needed; the member scratch is\n    // only needed when the member list does not fit in shared memory.\n    const size_t perBlockCandidates = static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int);\n    const size_t perBlock = perBlockCandidates +\n        (useShared ? 0 : static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int));\n\n    {\n        size_t freeMem = 0, totalMem = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemGetInfo(&freeMem, &totalMem));\n        const size_t budget = freeMem / 2;\n        long long fit = static_cast<long long>(budget / perBlock);\n        if (fit < 1) fit = 1;\n        if (fit < maxGrid) maxGrid = static_cast<int>(fit);\n        if (maxGrid < 1) maxGrid = 1;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratchCandidates,\n                              static_cast<size_t>(maxGrid) * perBlockCandidates));\n        if (!useShared) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scratchMembers,\n                                  static_cast<size_t>(maxGrid) *\n                                      static_cast<size_t>(N) * sizeof(int)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    int cur = 0;\n    int count = N;\n\n    while (count > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(d_bestKey, 0, sizeof(unsigned long long)));\n\n        const int grid = (count < maxGrid) ? count : maxGrid;\n        const int* seeds = d_uncl[cur];\n\n        if (useShared) {\n            candidatesKernel<true><<<grid, blockSize, sharedBytes>>>(\n                d_points, d_clustered, seeds, count, N, threshold,\n                d_cellStart, d_cellPoints, d_cellOf, gnx, gny,\n                nullptr, d_scratchCandidates, d_bestKey);\n            bestClusterKernel<true><<<1, blockSize, sharedBytes>>>(\n                d_points, d_clustered, seeds, N, threshold,\n                d_cellStart, d_cellPoints, d_cellOf, gnx, gny,\n                nullptr, d_scratchCandidates, d_bestKey,\n                d_bestMembers, d_memberCount, d_bestSeed);\n        } else {\n            candidatesKernel<false><<<grid, blockSize, sharedBytes>>>(\n                d_points, d_clustered, seeds, count, N, threshold,\n                d_cellStart, d_cellPoints, d_cellOf, gnx, gny,\n                d_scratchMembers, d_scratchCandidates, d_bestKey);\n            bestClusterKernel<false><<<1, blockSize, sharedBytes>>>(\n                d_points, d_clustered, seeds, N, threshold,\n                d_cellStart, d_cellPoints, d_cellOf, gnx, gny,\n                d_scratchMembers, d_scratchCandidates, d_bestKey,\n                d_bestMembers, d_memberCount, d_bestSeed);\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        int memberCount = 0;\n        int bestSeed = 0;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&memberCount, d_memberCount, sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&bestSeed, d_bestSeed, sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = bestSeed;\n        cluster.members.resize(memberCount);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(cluster.members.data(), d_bestMembers,\n                              static_cast<size_t>(memberCount) * sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        const int markBlocks =\n            std::min((memberCount + blockSize - 1) / blockSize, 1024);\n        markClusteredKernel<<<markBlocks < 1 ? 1 : markBlocks, blockSize>>>(\n            d_clustered, d_bestMembers, memberCount);\n\n        int* out = d_uncl[cur ^ 1];\n        compactUnclusteredKernel<<<1, 1024>>>(\n            d_clustered, seeds, count, out, d_newCount);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_newCount, sizeof(int),\n                              cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        cur ^= 1;\n    }\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_points));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_clustered));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_uncl[0]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_uncl[1]));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_bestMembers));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_memberCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_bestSeed));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_newCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_bestKey));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cellOf));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cellStart));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_cellPoints));\n    if (d_scratchMembers) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_scratchMembers));\n    if (d_scratchCandidates) CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(d_scratchCandidates));\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Calculate Euclidean distance between two points (host helper)\n    auto distance = [](const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n        const double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n        const double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n        return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n    };\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // The benchmark only ever uses the default device.  Restricting the\n    // visible device set to it avoids the cost of enumerating every GPU in\n    // the system when the CUDA context is first created.\n    if (getenv(\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\") == nullptr) {\n        setenv(\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\", \"0\", 0);\n    }\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\",\n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n\n    // Initialize the CUDA context before timing the clustering itself.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n\n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n\n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n\n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n\n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n\n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 :\n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n\n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n           total_clustered, num_points,\n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n\n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\",\n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/qtclustering_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_cuda_r2/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","time":{"start":1792484272584,"end":1792484272631}},"id":"prt_158635159001RITV41UCgTArfd","sessionID":"ses_ea7c2b7cfffeyQIxCBhxaJlWXY","messageID":"msg_15861bfe0001XHY9zSjun1zc2n"}}
